# Skills · W · part 7

841 entries, web-sqli to webgl-standards. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **web-sqli**: Detect and exploit SQL injection (error-based, UNION, boolean/time blind, stacked). Load when a param feeds a query, you see DB errors, numeric/string params change result sets, login forms, search, sort/order-by, or ORM raw queries. Signals: "id=", 500 on a quote, "You have an error in your SQL syntax", MySQL/Postgres/MSSQL/Oracle banners. ([NoorQureshi/SploitAgent](https://github.com/NoorQureshi/SploitAgent/tree/HEAD/skills/web/web-sqli) · ★18)
- **web-sre**: Reviews release readiness, verifies post-release deployments, and diagnoses production incidents for web services with hypothesis-driven, approval-gated probing and upstream-status-first checks. Use this whenever the user is shipping a release, verifying a deploy, investigating a production issue, asks 'is something broken in production', or asks for an SRE or incident review — even if they only describe symptoms like '5xx spiked'. ([sigmadigitalza/engineering-playbook](https://github.com/sigmadigitalza/engineering-playbook/tree/HEAD/plugins/sigma-engineering/skills/web-sre) · ★1)
- **web-ssrf**: Discover and escalate Server-Side Request Forgery. Load when the app fetches a URL you influence: webhooks, "import from URL", link/image preview, PDF/HTML render, avatar-by-URL, URL health-checks, XML/SVG parsers. Signals: params like url=, uri=, dest=, callback=, image=, feed=, or a request that reaches out on your behalf. ([NoorQureshi/SploitAgent](https://github.com/NoorQureshi/SploitAgent/tree/HEAD/skills/web/web-ssrf) · ★18)
- **web-ssrf-gopher-redis-rce**: Turn a server-side request (SSRF) into RCE by speaking the Redis protocol over gopher:// to an internal, unauthenticated Redis — write a cron job, an SSH key, or a webshell. Load when: SSRF is confirmed (URL fetch, webhook, PDF/URL preview, image proxy) AND an internal Redis/6379 (or similar line-protocol service) is reachable. Authorized targets only. ([NoorQureshi/SploitAgent](https://github.com/NoorQureshi/SploitAgent/tree/HEAD/skills/web/web-ssrf-gopher-redis-rce) · ★18)
- **web-ssti**: Server-Side Template Injection → RCE. Load when user input is rendered by a template engine: profile names in emails, custom reports, "hello {{name}}", error pages echoing math, Jinja2/Twig/Freemarker/Velocity/ERB/Handlebars. Signals: {{7*7}} returns 49, ${...} or #{...} evaluated, framework stack traces mentioning a templating engine. ([NoorQureshi/SploitAgent](https://github.com/NoorQureshi/SploitAgent/tree/HEAD/skills/web/web-ssti) · ★18)
- **web-stack-guardrails**: Correctness, security, and current-API guardrails for the Node/Mongo/React stack. Use when writing or reviewing server-side code (Node, Express, NestJS, Mongoose/TypeORM), UI (React, Next.js, EJS, Tailwind), background work (BullMQ, cron, scrapers, bots), payment or credit logic, LLM integrations, or deployment config. Catches deprecated APIs models reproduce from stale training data, IDOR and injection, N+1 and race conditions, at-least-once delivery bugs, and broken responsive/RTL/a11y patterns. Read SKILL.md, then load only the reference file matching the layer being touched.
